## r1130 (归档批次 r1131, 2026-11-25) 完整流水

发现 22:26 的 status=? 是初始 Lite 清单批量盖戳, 非真实 per-case verify. 用忠实复刻 run_one.sh L363-470 judge-tree 的 reverify 脚本 (/tmp/reverify_c2.py: 建 JUDGE/hidden + g1/g2 + symlink 树 + A 附件 + reference_submit.zip, 跑 check.py + negative_selftest.py) 逐条实跑. 结果: 1 真 P0 — DrcPatCheckAudit-001 reference_submit.zip MISSING (refdir 只有 drc_pattern_check_audit.py 未打包), 已用 zip 打包 (§4.1 packaging fix, 不改题/断言), post-fix REF check()=>True PASS + 4neg all-FAIL distinct {c,b,l}=3, commit EDA-Flow-Bench e8c92e1ee. 5 条 confirmed true-green: DdgoaDeviceAudit 12/12+5neg{b,c,d}; DeckIncludeResolve +5neg{a,e,p,j}; DrcLogAnalyzer +6neg{c,o,j,m,i,l} fully-distinct; DrcMatchRca +negselftest all-FAIL; DrcPairWaiveClassify +5neg distinct=5. 2 个 L4 false-alarm (DrcLogAnalyzer/DrcPairWaiveClassify crude gui-grep 命中 'calibre' 但实为 forbidden-imports 字符串列表 / json 文件变量名, 非二进制调用, L4 OK). 1 个 harness-bug false-alarm (DeckIncludeResolve 初测 L2=FAIL 因我的 hidden 子目录未递归拷贝, 改 copytree dirs_exist_ok 后 PASS). cross_shard 仍 pending B: OpampChar-001 (check.py hspice-direct 0-mock, ref_submit EXISTS) + InvTripDelay-001 (check.py hspice-direct 0-mock, ref_submit MISSING) — B r1208/1209 已修 SpiceSim/PlatInvsThick/SpiceTbGen 但未触这 2 条. P1 DEBT 不变: StarrcReleaseNoteGen-001 L3 3-neg all first-fail cond b. 三假-distinct 口径 DEBT 仍待 zhulong/A 裁定. 教训: (1) 22:26 批量盖戳 status=? 不可信, 须逐条实跑; (2) L4 gui-grep 须区分二进制调用 vs 字符串/变量名; (3) reverify judge-tree 须递归拷贝 hidden 子目录. 净产出: 1 P0 fixed (DrcPatCheckAudit) + 5 true-green confirmed + reverify 脚本沉淀. md5=f7816ef4137353904e40a1a3b01fb3ae (500 行) 本 shard Lite=137 unchanged. 📦 体积: TASK≈30KB / MEMORY≈27KB. 下轮: 继续 22:26 cohort fresh re-verify (剩 ~125 case 仍 stale) + 关注 B 2 hspice-mock P0 + 等 zhulong/A 裁定三假-distinct 口径.
